```json
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "$id": "https://contracts.sikkerheit.example.invalid/agent/observation.schema.json",
  "title": "Sikkerheit Agent Observation",
  "type": "object",
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "properties": {
    "checkId": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "WIN-001",
        "WIN-002",
        "WIN-003",
        "WIN-004",
        "WIN-005",
        "WIN-006",
        "WIN-007",
        "WIN-008",
        "WIN-009",
        "WIN-010",
        "WIN-011"
      ]
    },
    "collectorVersion": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 40,
      "pattern": "^[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+(?:[-+][A-Za-z0-9.-]+)?$"
    },
    "subject": {
      "type": "object",
      "description": "Форма объекта задаётся по checkId (allOf)."
    },
    "observedAt": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 40,
      "format": "date-time",
      "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?Z$"
    },
    "collectionStatus": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "collected",
        "unavailable"
      ]
    },
    "evidence": {
      "type": "object"
    },
    "reasonCode": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "permission_denied",
        "unsupported",
        "collector_timeout",
        "temporary_error"
      ]
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "checkId",
    "collectorVersion",
    "subject",
    "observedAt",
    "collectionStatus"
  ],
  "oneOf": [
    {
      "properties": {
        "collectionStatus": {
          "const": "collected"
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "evidence"
      ],
      "not": {
        "required": [
          "reasonCode"
        ]
      }
    },
    {
      "properties": {
        "collectionStatus": {
          "const": "unavailable"
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "reasonCode"
      ],
      "not": {
        "required": [
          "evidence"
        ]
      }
    }
  ],
  "allOf": [
    {
      "if": {
        "properties": {
          "checkId": {
            "const": "WIN-001"
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "checkId"
        ]
      },
      "then": {
        "properties": {
          "subject": {
            "type": "object",
            "additionalProperties": false,
            "properties": {
              "type": {
                "const": "volume"
              },
              "volumeId": {
                "type": "string",
                "minLength": 1,
                "maxLength": 64,
                "pattern": "^\\\\\\\\\\?\\\\Volume\\{[0-9a-fA-F-]{36}\\}\\\\$"
              },
              "mountPoint": {
                "type": "string",
                "minLength": 1,
                "maxLength": 2,
                "pattern": "^[A-Z]:$"
              },
              "role": {
                "const": "os"
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "type",
              "volumeId",
              "role"
            ]
          }
        }
      }
    },
    {
      "if": {
        "properties": {
          "checkId": {
            "const": "WIN-002"
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "checkId"
        ]
      },
      "then": {
        "properties": {
          "subject": {
            "type": "object",
            "additionalProperties": false,
            "properties": {
              "type": {
                "const": "volume"
              },
              "volumeId": {
                "type": "string",
                "minLength": 1,
                "maxLength": 64,
                "pattern": "^\\\\\\\\\\?\\\\Volume\\{[0-9a-fA-F-]{36}\\}\\\\$"
              },
              "mountPoint": {
                "type": "string",
                "minLength": 1,
                "maxLength": 2,
                "pattern": "^[A-Z]:$"
              },
              "role": {
                "const": "os"
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "type",
              "volumeId",
              "role"
            ]
          }
        }
      }
    },
    {
      "if": {
        "properties": {
          "checkId": {
            "const": "WIN-003"
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "checkId"
        ]
      },
      "then": {
        "properties": {
          "subject": {
            "type": "object",
            "additionalProperties": false,
            "properties": {
              "type": {
                "const": "device"
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "type"
            ]
          }
        }
      }
    },
    {
      "if": {
        "properties": {
          "checkId": {
            "const": "WIN-004"
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "checkId"
        ]
      },
      "then": {
        "properties": {
          "subject": {
            "type": "object",
            "additionalProperties": false,
            "properties": {
              "type": {
                "const": "device"
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "type"
            ]
          }
        }
      }
    },
    {
      "if": {
        "properties": {
          "checkId": {
            "const": "WIN-005"
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "checkId"
        ]
      },
      "then": {
        "properties": {
          "subject": {
            "type": "object",
            "additionalProperties": false,
            "properties": {
              "type": {
                "const": "device"
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "type"
            ]
          }
        }
      }
    },
    {
      "if": {
        "properties": {
          "checkId": {
            "const": "WIN-006"
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "checkId"
        ]
      },
      "then": {
        "properties": {
          "subject": {
            "type": "object",
            "additionalProperties": false,
            "properties": {
              "type": {
                "const": "device"
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "type"
            ]
          }
        }
      }
    },
    {
      "if": {
        "properties": {
          "checkId": {
            "const": "WIN-007"
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "checkId"
        ]
      },
      "then": {
        "properties": {
          "subject": {
            "type": "object",
            "additionalProperties": false,
            "properties": {
              "type": {
                "const": "device"
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "type"
            ]
          }
        }
      }
    },
    {
      "if": {
        "properties": {
          "checkId": {
            "const": "WIN-008"
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "checkId"
        ]
      },
      "then": {
        "properties": {
          "subject": {
            "type": "object",
            "additionalProperties": false,
            "properties": {
              "type": {
                "const": "device"
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "type"
            ]
          }
        }
      }
    },
    {
      "if": {
        "properties": {
          "checkId": {
            "const": "WIN-009"
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "checkId"
        ]
      },
      "then": {
        "properties": {
          "subject": {
            "type": "object",
            "additionalProperties": false,
            "properties": {
              "type": {
                "const": "device"
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "type"
            ]
          }
        }
      }
    },
    {
      "if": {
        "properties": {
          "checkId": {
            "const": "WIN-010"
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "checkId"
        ]
      },
      "then": {
        "properties": {
          "subject": {
            "type": "object",
            "additionalProperties": false,
            "properties": {
              "type": {
                "const": "device"
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "type"
            ]
          }
        }
      }
    },
    {
      "if": {
        "properties": {
          "checkId": {
            "const": "WIN-011"
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "checkId"
        ]
      },
      "then": {
        "properties": {
          "subject": {
            "type": "object",
            "additionalProperties": false,
            "properties": {
              "type": {
                "const": "device"
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "type"
            ]
          }
        }
      }
    },
    {
      "if": {
        "properties": {
          "checkId": {
            "const": "WIN-001"
          },
          "collectionStatus": {
            "const": "collected"
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "checkId",
          "collectionStatus"
        ]
      },
      "then": {
        "properties": {
          "evidence": {
            "type": "object",
            "additionalProperties": false,
            "properties": {
              "totalBytes": {
                "type": "integer",
                "minimum": 1
              },
              "freeBytes": {
                "type": "integer",
                "minimum": 0
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "totalBytes",
              "freeBytes"
            ]
          }
        }
      }
    },
    {
      "if": {
        "properties": {
          "checkId": {
            "const": "WIN-002"
          },
          "collectionStatus": {
            "const": "collected"
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "checkId",
          "collectionStatus"
        ]
      },
      "then": {
        "properties": {
          "evidence": {
            "type": "object",
            "additionalProperties": false,
            "properties": {
              "volumeStatus": {
                "type": "string",
                "enum": [
                  "fully_encrypted",
                  "encrypting",
                  "decrypting",
                  "fully_decrypted",
                  "encryption_paused",
                  "decryption_paused"
                ]
              },
              "protectionStatus": {
                "type": "string",
                "enum": [
                  "on",
                  "off",
                  "unknown"
                ]
              },
              "encryptionPercentage": {
                "type": "integer",
                "minimum": 0,
                "maximum": 100
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "volumeStatus",
              "protectionStatus",
              "encryptionPercentage"
            ]
          }
        }
      }
    },
    {
      "if": {
        "properties": {
          "checkId": {
            "const": "WIN-003"
          },
          "collectionStatus": {
            "const": "collected"
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "checkId",
          "collectionStatus"
        ]
      },
      "then": {
        "properties": {
          "evidence": {
            "type": "object",
            "additionalProperties": false,
            "properties": {
              "products": {
                "type": "array",
                "items": {
                  "type": "object",
                  "additionalProperties": false,
                  "properties": {
                    "provider": {
                      "type": "string",
                      "minLength": 1,
                      "maxLength": 120,
                      "pattern": "^[^\\u0000-\\u001F\\u007F-\\u009F​-‏‪-‮⁦-⁩<>]+$"
                    },
                    "productState": {
                      "type": "string",
                      "enum": [
                        "on",
                        "off",
                        "snoozed",
                        "expired",
                        "unknown"
                      ]
                    },
                    "statusSource": {
                      "type": "string",
                      "enum": [
                        "windows_security_center",
                        "defender"
                      ]
                    },
                    "rawState": {
                      "type": [
                        "integer",
                        "null"
                      ]
                    }
                  },
                  "required": [
                    "provider",
                    "productState",
                    "statusSource"
                  ],
                  "allOf": [
                    {
                      "if": {
                        "properties": {
                          "productState": {
                            "const": "unknown"
                          }
                        },
                        "required": [
                          "productState"
                        ]
                      },
                      "then": {
                        "required": [
                          "rawState"
                        ],
                        "properties": {
                          "rawState": {
                            "type": "integer"
                          }
                        }
                      }
                    }
                  ]
                },
                "minItems": 0,
                "maxItems": 10,
                "description": "Больше 10 продуктов: collectionStatus=unavailable, reasonCode=unsupported; список не усекается."
              },
              "defender": {
                "type": [
                  "object",
                  "null"
                ],
                "additionalProperties": false,
                "properties": {
                  "amRunningMode": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "enum": [
                      "normal",
                      "passive",
                      "edr_block",
                      "sxs_passive",
                      "not_running",
                      "other"
                    ]
                  },
                  "realTimeProtectionEnabled": {
                    "type": "boolean"
                  }
                },
                "required": [
                  "amRunningMode",
                  "realTimeProtectionEnabled"
                ]
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "products",
              "defender"
            ]
          }
        }
      }
    },
    {
      "if": {
        "properties": {
          "checkId": {
            "const": "WIN-004"
          },
          "collectionStatus": {
            "const": "collected"
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "checkId",
          "collectionStatus"
        ]
      },
      "then": {
        "properties": {
          "evidence": {
            "type": "object",
            "additionalProperties": false,
            "properties": {
              "signatureLastUpdatedAt": {
                "type": "string",
                "minLength": 1,
                "maxLength": 40,
                "format": "date-time",
                "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?Z$"
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "signatureLastUpdatedAt"
            ]
          }
        }
      }
    },
    {
      "if": {
        "properties": {
          "checkId": {
            "const": "WIN-005"
          },
          "collectionStatus": {
            "const": "collected"
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "checkId",
          "collectionStatus"
        ]
      },
      "then": {
        "properties": {
          "evidence": {
            "type": "object",
            "additionalProperties": false,
            "properties": {
              "domainEnabled": {
                "type": "boolean"
              },
              "privateEnabled": {
                "type": "boolean"
              },
              "publicEnabled": {
                "type": "boolean"
              },
              "firewallProducts": {
                "type": "array",
                "items": {
                  "type": "object",
                  "additionalProperties": false,
                  "properties": {
                    "provider": {
                      "type": "string",
                      "minLength": 1,
                      "maxLength": 120,
                      "pattern": "^[^\\u0000-\\u001F\\u007F-\\u009F​-‏‪-‮⁦-⁩<>]+$"
                    },
                    "productState": {
                      "type": "string",
                      "enum": [
                        "on",
                        "off",
                        "snoozed",
                        "expired",
                        "unknown"
                      ]
                    }
                  },
                  "required": [
                    "provider",
                    "productState"
                  ]
                },
                "minItems": 0,
                "maxItems": 10,
                "description": "Сторонние firewall из Windows Security Center."
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "domainEnabled",
              "privateEnabled",
              "publicEnabled",
              "firewallProducts"
            ]
          }
        }
      }
    },
    {
      "if": {
        "properties": {
          "checkId": {
            "const": "WIN-006"
          },
          "collectionStatus": {
            "const": "collected"
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "checkId",
          "collectionStatus"
        ]
      },
      "then": {
        "properties": {
          "evidence": {
            "type": "object",
            "additionalProperties": false,
            "properties": {
              "lastSearchSuccessAt": {
                "type": [
                  "string",
                  "null"
                ],
                "minLength": 1,
                "maxLength": 40,
                "format": "date-time",
                "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?Z$",
                "description": "LastSearchSuccessDate службы автоматического обновления; null — нет."
              },
              "lastInstallSuccessAt": {
                "type": [
                  "string",
                  "null"
                ],
                "minLength": 1,
                "maxLength": 40,
                "format": "date-time",
                "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?Z$",
                "description": "LastInstallationSuccessDate; null — нет."
              },
              "wsusConfigured": {
                "type": "boolean",
                "description": "Политика задаёт сервер WSUS (WUServer и UseWUServer)."
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "lastSearchSuccessAt",
              "lastInstallSuccessAt",
              "wsusConfigured"
            ]
          }
        }
      }
    },
    {
      "if": {
        "properties": {
          "checkId": {
            "const": "WIN-007"
          },
          "collectionStatus": {
            "const": "collected"
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "checkId",
          "collectionStatus"
        ]
      },
      "then": {
        "properties": {
          "evidence": {
            "type": "object",
            "additionalProperties": false,
            "properties": {
              "rebootRequired": {
                "type": "boolean"
              },
              "lastBootAt": {
                "type": "string",
                "minLength": 1,
                "maxLength": 40,
                "format": "date-time",
                "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?Z$"
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "rebootRequired",
              "lastBootAt"
            ]
          }
        }
      }
    },
    {
      "if": {
        "properties": {
          "checkId": {
            "const": "WIN-008"
          },
          "collectionStatus": {
            "const": "collected"
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "checkId",
          "collectionStatus"
        ]
      },
      "then": {
        "properties": {
          "evidence": {
            "type": "object",
            "additionalProperties": false,
            "properties": {
              "members": {
                "type": "array",
                "items": {
                  "type": "object",
                  "additionalProperties": false,
                  "properties": {
                    "sid": {
                      "type": "string",
                      "minLength": 1,
                      "maxLength": 184,
                      "pattern": "^S-[0-9]+(?:-[0-9]+)+$"
                    },
                    "kind": {
                      "type": "string",
                      "enum": [
                        "builtin_administrator",
                        "local_account",
                        "domain",
                        "entra",
                        "well_known",
                        "unknown"
                      ]
                    },
                    "relativeId": {
                      "type": [
                        "integer",
                        "null"
                      ],
                      "minimum": 0,
                      "description": "RID относительно SID машины или домена; null для entra и well_known."
                    }
                  },
                  "required": [
                    "sid",
                    "kind",
                    "relativeId"
                  ]
                },
                "minItems": 0,
                "maxItems": 100,
                "uniqueItems": true
              },
              "membershipScope": {
                "type": "string",
                "enum": [
                  "direct_members"
                ]
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "members",
              "membershipScope"
            ]
          }
        }
      }
    },
    {
      "if": {
        "properties": {
          "checkId": {
            "const": "WIN-009"
          },
          "collectionStatus": {
            "const": "collected"
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "checkId",
          "collectionStatus"
        ]
      },
      "then": {
        "properties": {
          "evidence": {
            "type": "object",
            "additionalProperties": false,
            "properties": {
              "enabled": {
                "type": "boolean"
              },
              "platform": {
                "type": "string",
                "enum": [
                  "uefi",
                  "legacy_bios"
                ]
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "enabled",
              "platform"
            ]
          }
        }
      }
    },
    {
      "if": {
        "properties": {
          "checkId": {
            "const": "WIN-010"
          },
          "collectionStatus": {
            "const": "collected"
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "checkId",
          "collectionStatus"
        ]
      },
      "then": {
        "properties": {
          "evidence": {
            "type": "object",
            "additionalProperties": false,
            "properties": {
              "joinType": {
                "type": "string",
                "enum": [
                  "entra_joined",
                  "hybrid_joined",
                  "domain_joined",
                  "not_joined"
                ]
              },
              "tenantId": {
                "type": [
                  "string",
                  "null"
                ],
                "minLength": 1,
                "maxLength": 36,
                "format": "uuid",
                "pattern": "^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$"
              },
              "entraDeviceId": {
                "type": [
                  "string",
                  "null"
                ],
                "minLength": 1,
                "maxLength": 36,
                "format": "uuid",
                "pattern": "^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$"
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "joinType",
              "tenantId",
              "entraDeviceId"
            ]
          }
        }
      }
    },
    {
      "if": {
        "properties": {
          "checkId": {
            "const": "WIN-011"
          },
          "collectionStatus": {
            "const": "collected"
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "checkId",
          "collectionStatus"
        ]
      },
      "then": {
        "properties": {
          "evidence": {
            "type": "object",
            "additionalProperties": false,
            "properties": {
              "editionId": {
                "type": "string",
                "minLength": 1,
                "maxLength": 64,
                "pattern": "^[A-Za-z]+$"
              },
              "displayVersion": {
                "type": [
                  "string",
                  "null"
                ],
                "minLength": 1,
                "maxLength": 8,
                "pattern": "^([0-9]{2}H[0-9]|[0-9]{4})$",
                "description": "DisplayVersion, у старых сборок — ReleaseId; null, если нет обоих."
              },
              "build": {
                "type": "integer",
                "minimum": 1
              },
              "ubr": {
                "type": "integer",
                "minimum": 0
              },
              "architecture": {
                "type": "string",
                "enum": [
                  "x64",
                  "arm64"
                ]
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "editionId",
              "displayVersion",
              "build",
              "ubr",
              "architecture"
            ]
          }
        }
      }
    }
  ],
  "description": "Закрытые allowlisted evidence-структуры агента для каждой WIN-проверки. Применимость определяет сервер: агент не передаёт not_applicable. Межполевые и семантические инварианты (freeBytes <= totalBytes, ожидаемые объекты, время) проверяет backend по 05-data-and-api.md и specs/invariants.json."
}
```

```json
              "type": "integer",

            "name": "freshness",

              "type": "string",

                "M365-004",
                "M365-005",
                "M365-006",
                "M365-007",

                "WIN-003",

                "WIN-005",
                "WIN-006",
                "WIN-007",
                "WIN-008",
                "WIN-009",
                "WIN-010",

                "SYS-001",
                "SYS-002",
                "SYS-003"
              ]
            }
          },
          {
            "name": "workflowState",
            "in": "query",
            "style": "form",
            "explode": false,
            "schema": {
              "type": "array",
              "items": {
                "type": "string",
                "enum": [
                  "new",
                  "in_progress",
                  "risk_accepted",
                  "suppressed"
                ]
              },
              "minItems": 1,
              "maxItems": 4,
              "uniqueItems": true
            },
            "description": "Несколько значений через запятую, например workflowState=new,in_progress. Очередь по умолчанию — technicalState=open&workflowState=new,in_progress."
          },
          {
            "name": "Accept-Language",
            "in": "header",
            "required": false,
            "schema": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 35
            },
            "description": "Язык title, recommendation и explanation. Принимаются nb, nn и en; без заголовка или при любом другом значении используется nb, ошибки нет."
          }
        ]
      }
    },

                "M365-004",
                "M365-005",
                "M365-006",
                "M365-007",
                "WIN-001",
                "WIN-002",
                "WIN-003",
                "WIN-004",
                "WIN-005",
                "WIN-006",
                "WIN-007",
                "WIN-008",
                "WIN-009",
                "WIN-010",
                "WIN-011",
                "SYS-001",
                "SYS-002",
                "SYS-003"
              ]
            }
          },
          {
            "name": "workflowState",
            "in": "query",
            "style": "form",
            "explode": false,
            "schema": {
              "type": "array",
              "items": {
                "type": "string",
                "enum": [
                  "new",
                  "in_progress",
                  "risk_accepted",
                  "suppressed"
                ]
              },
              "minItems": 1,
              "maxItems": 4,
              "uniqueItems": true
            },
            "description": "Несколько значений через запятую, например workflowState=new,in_progress. Очередь по умолчанию — technicalState=open&workflowState=new,in_progress."

          {
            "name": "Accept-Language",
            "in": "header",
            "required": false,
            "schema": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 35

          }

      }

                }

                "schema": {
                  "type": "integer",
                  "minimum": 1
                },
                "description": "Seconds before retry."
              }
            }
          }
        },
        "description": "Ответ 200 после записи и оценки в одной транзакции; порядок — 05, «Порядок приёма пакета».",
        "requestBody": {
          "required": true,
          "content": {
            "application/json": {
              "schema": {
                "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ScanBatch"
              }
            }
          }
        },
        "parameters": [
          {
            "name": "Idempotency-Key",
            "in": "header",
            "required": true,
            "schema": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 36,
              "format": "uuid",
              "pattern": "^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$"
            },
            "description": "Равен scanId; повтор тех же байтов возвращает исходный receipt."
          },
          {
            "name": "Sikkerheit-Sent-At",
            "in": "header",
            "required": true,
            "schema": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 40,
              "format": "date-time",
              "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?Z$"
            },
            "description": "Время ПК в момент этой попытки; не входит в тело и payloadHash. По нему считаются возраст пакета и признак часов."
          }
        ],
        "servers": [
          {
            "url": "https://agent.example.invalid",
            "description": "Hostname агента того же сервиса"
          }
        ]
      }
    }
  },
  "components": {
    "securitySchemes": {
      "PortalSession": {
        "type": "apiKey",
        "in": "cookie",
        "name": "__Host-sikkerheit-session",
        "description": "Серверная OIDC-сессия сотрудника (Entra ID компании), HttpOnly Secure SameSite=Lax; роль из app roles проверяется на каждом запросе."
      },
      "CsrfToken": {
        "type": "apiKey",
        "in": "header",
        "name": "X-CSRF-Token",
        "description": "Обязателен вместе с PortalSession для изменений; значение — CurrentStaff.csrfToken."
      },
      "EnrollmentKey": {
        "type": "apiKey",
        "in": "header",
        "name": "Authorization",
        "description": "Enrollment <key>: ключ регистрации организации; хранится на сервере только SHA-256, в логи не пишется (04, «Ключ регистрации организации»)."
      },
      "AgentSession": {
        "type": "http",
        "scheme": "bearer",
        "description": "Сессионный токен устройства на 60 минут, полученный createAgentSession по подписи ключа устройства. Сервер хранит SHA-256 токена и на каждом запросе проверяет срок, статус устройства и организации по БД, без кэша."
      }
    },
    "schemas": {
      "ScanBatch": {
        "title": "Sikkerheit Agent Scan Batch",
        "type": "object",
        "additionalProperties": false,
        "properties": {
          "schemaVersion": {
            "const": "2.0"
          },
          "scanId": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 36,
            "format": "uuid",
            "pattern": "^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$"
          },
          "sequence": {
            "type": "integer",
            "minimum": 1
          },
          "agentVersion": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 40,
            "pattern": "^[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+(?:[-+][A-Za-z0-9.-]+)?$"
          },
          "policyVersion": {
            "type": "integer",
            "minimum": 1,
            "description": "Версия политики источника."
          },
          "machineFingerprint": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 64,
            "pattern": "^[0-9a-f]{64}$"
          },
          "startedAt": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 40,
            "format": "date-time",
            "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?Z$"
          },
          "completedAt": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 40,
            "format": "date-time",
            "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?Z$"
          },
          "scanRequestId": {
            "type": [
              "string",
              "null"
            ],
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 36,
            "format": "uuid",
            "pattern": "^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$",
            "description": "Чужой или просроченный scanRequestId не отклоняет пакет: он принимается как обычный сбор, scanRequestId игнорируется, пишется запись диагностики."
          },
          "observations": {
            "type": "array",
            "items": {
              "$ref": "#/components/schemas/Observation"
            },
            "minItems": 1,
            "maxItems": 200,
            "description": "Ключ (checkId, subject.type, localKey) уникален внутри пакета; localKey = device для устройства и volumeId для тома. Повтор ключа: 422 duplicate_observation."
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "schemaVersion",
          "scanId",
          "sequence",
          "agentVersion",
          "policyVersion",
          "machineFingerprint",
          "startedAt",
          "completedAt",
          "observations"
        ],
        "description": "DeviceId и OrganizationId определяются сессией устройства, а не телом пакета. Лимит байтов, idempotency, sequence, окна и время проверяются сервером дополнительно (05, «Порядок приёма пакета»). Пакет с completedAt раньше startedAt или observedAt вне [startedAt, completedAt] отклоняется 422 batch_time_inconsistent. Время отправки передаётся заголовком Sikkerheit-Sent-At каждой попытки и в тело не входит, поэтому повтор отправляет те же байты (05, «Время и часы устройства»)."
      },
      "Observation": {
        "title": "Sikkerheit Agent Observation",
        "type": "object",
        "additionalProperties": false,
        "properties": {
          "checkId": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "WIN-001",
              "WIN-002",
              "WIN-003",
              "WIN-004",
              "WIN-005",
              "WIN-006",
              "WIN-007",
              "WIN-008",
              "WIN-009",
              "WIN-010",
              "WIN-011"
            ]
          },
          "collectorVersion": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 40,
            "pattern": "^[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+(?:[-+][A-Za-z0-9.-]+)?$"
          },
          "subject": {
            "type": "object",
            "description": "Форма объекта задаётся по checkId (allOf)."
          },
          "observedAt": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 40,
            "format": "date-time",
            "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?Z$"
          },
          "collectionStatus": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "collected",
              "unavailable"
            ]
          },
          "evidence": {
            "type": "object"
          },
          "reasonCode": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "permission_denied",
              "unsupported",
              "collector_timeout",
              "temporary_error"
            ]
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "checkId",
          "collectorVersion",
          "subject",
          "observedAt",
          "collectionStatus"
        ],
        "oneOf": [
          {
            "properties": {
              "collectionStatus": {
                "const": "collected"
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "evidence"
            ],
            "not": {
              "required": [
                "reasonCode"
              ]
            }
          },
          {
            "properties": {
              "collectionStatus": {
                "const": "unavailable"
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "reasonCode"
            ],
            "not": {
              "required": [
                "evidence"
              ]
            }
          }
        ],
        "allOf": [
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "checkId": {
                  "const": "WIN-001"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "checkId"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "subject": {
                  "type": "object",
                  "additionalProperties": false,
                  "properties": {
                    "type": {
                      "const": "volume"
                    },
                    "volumeId": {
                      "type": "string",
                      "minLength": 1,
                      "maxLength": 64,
                      "pattern": "^\\\\\\\\\\?\\\\Volume\\{[0-9a-fA-F-]{36}\\}\\\\$"
                    },
                    "mountPoint": {
                      "type": "string",
                      "minLength": 1,
                      "maxLength": 2,
                      "pattern": "^[A-Z]:$"
                    },
                    "role": {
                      "const": "os"
                    }
                  },
                  "required": [
                    "type",
                    "volumeId",
                    "role"
                  ]
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "checkId": {
                  "const": "WIN-002"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "checkId"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "subject": {
                  "type": "object",
                  "additionalProperties": false,
                  "properties": {
                    "type": {
                      "const": "volume"
                    },
                    "volumeId": {
                      "type": "string",
                      "minLength": 1,
                      "maxLength": 64,
                      "pattern": "^\\\\\\\\\\?\\\\Volume\\{[0-9a-fA-F-]{36}\\}\\\\$"
                    },
                    "mountPoint": {
                      "type": "string",
                      "minLength": 1,
                      "maxLength": 2,
                      "pattern": "^[A-Z]:$"
                    },
                    "role": {
                      "const": "os"
                    }
                  },
                  "required": [
                    "type",
                    "volumeId",
                    "role"
                  ]
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "checkId": {
                  "const": "WIN-003"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "checkId"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "subject": {
                  "type": "object",
                  "additionalProperties": false,
                  "properties": {
                    "type": {
                      "const": "device"
                    }
                  },
                  "required": [
                    "type"
                  ]
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "checkId": {
                  "const": "WIN-004"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "checkId"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "subject": {
                  "type": "object",
                  "additionalProperties": false,
                  "properties": {
                    "type": {
                      "const": "device"
                    }
                  },
                  "required": [
                    "type"
                  ]
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "checkId": {
                  "const": "WIN-005"
                }

        "description": "Закрытые allowlisted evidence-структуры агента для каждой WIN-проверки. Применимость определяет сервер: агент не передаёт not_applicable. Межполевые и семантические инварианты (freeBytes <= totalBytes, ожидаемые объекты, время) проверяет backend по 05-data-and-api.md и specs/invariants.json."
      },
      "Problem": {
        "type": "object",
        "additionalProperties": false,
        "properties": {
          "type": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 500
          },
          "title": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 200
          },
          "status": {
            "type": "integer",
            "minimum": 400,
            "maximum": 599
          },
          "code": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "invalid_json",
              "unsupported_media_type",
              "payload_too_large",
              "schema_violation",
              "unsupported_schema_version",
              "unexpected_subject",
              "duplicate_observation",
              "unknown_policy_version",
              "batch_too_old",
              "batch_time_inconsistent",
              "agent_version_unsupported",
              "idempotency_conflict",
              "sequence_conflict",
              "identity_conflict",
              "organization_inactive",
              "enrollment_key_invalid",
              "challenge_invalid",
              "session_invalid",
              "device_unknown",
              "device_revoked",
              "rate_limited",
              "temporarily_unavailable",
              "session_required",
              "csrf_failed",
              "forbidden",
              "not_found",
              "invalid_cursor",
              "revision_mismatch",
              "if_match_required",
              "workflow_transition_forbidden",
              "lifecycle_transition_forbidden",
              "connection_state_conflict",
              "tenant_conflict",
              "consent_not_granted",
              "resource_not_ready",
              "validation_failed",
              "policy_inputs_missing"
            ]
          },
          "correlationId": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 100
          },
          "detail": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 1000
          },
          "instance": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 500
          },
          "minimumAgentVersion": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 40,
            "pattern": "^[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+(?:[-+][A-Za-z0-9.-]+)?$"
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "type",
          "title",
          "status",
          "code",
          "correlationId"
        ]
      },
      "Capability": {
        "type": "object",
        "additionalProperties": false,
        "properties": {
          "module": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "tenant",
              "licenses",
              "users",
              "auth_methods",
              "roles",
              "security_defaults",
              "conditional_access",
              "authorization_policy",
              "sign_in_activity",
              "per_user_mfa"
            ]
          },
          "status": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "available",
              "skipped",
              "permission_missing",
              "license_missing",
              "partial",
              "temporary_error"
            ]
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "module",
          "status"
        ]
      },
      "ChallengeRequest": {
        "type": "object",
        "additionalProperties": false,
        "properties": {
          "purpose": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "enroll",
              "session"
            ]
          },
          "deviceId": {
            "type": [
              "string",
              "null"
            ],
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 36,
            "format": "uuid",
            "pattern": "^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$"
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "purpose"
        ],
        "allOf": [
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "purpose": {
                  "const": "session"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "purpose"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "required": [
                "deviceId"
              ],
              "properties": {
                "deviceId": {
                  "type": "string",
                  "minLength": 1,
                  "maxLength": 36,
                  "format": "uuid",
                  "pattern": "^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$"
                }
              }
            },
            "else": {
              "not": {
                "required": [
                  "deviceId"
                ]
              }
            }
          }
        ]
      },
      "Challenge": {
        "type": "object",
        "additionalProperties": false,
        "properties": {
          "nonce": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 43,
            "maxLength": 43,
            "pattern": "^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43}$",
            "description": "32 байта в base64url без дополнения: 12 случайных, 4 байта срока и 16 байт HMAC сервера; одноразовый, 5 минут (05, «Регистрация и сессия»)."
          },

            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 40,
            "format": "date-time",
            "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?Z$"
          }
        },

      "EnrollmentRequest": {
        "type": "object",
        "additionalProperties": false,
        "properties": {
          "registrationRequestId": {
            "type": "string",

          },
          "installationId": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 36,
            "format": "uuid",
            "pattern": "^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$"
          },
          "displayName": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 120,
            "pattern": "^[^\\u0000-\\u001F\\u007F-\\u009F​-‏‪-‮⁦-⁩<>]+$"
          },
          "machineFingerprint": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 64,
            "pattern": "^[0-9a-f]{64}$"
          },
          "publicKey": {
            "$ref": "#/components/schemas/PublicKeyJwk"
          },
          "keyProtection": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "tpm",
              "software"
            ]
          },
          "nonce": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 43,
            "maxLength": 43,
            "pattern": "^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43}$",
            "description": "32 байта в base64url без дополнения: 12 случайных, 4 байта срока и 16 байт HMAC сервера; одноразовый, 5 минут (05, «Регистрация и сессия»)."
          },
          "signature": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 86,
            "maxLength": 86,
            "pattern": "^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{86}$",
            "description": "ECDSA P-256 SHA-256 в формате IEEE P1363 (r||s, 64 байта), base64url."
          },
          "previousKeySignature": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 86,
            "maxLength": 86,
            "pattern": "^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{86}$",
            "description": "ECDSA P-256 SHA-256 в формате IEEE P1363 (r||s, 64 байта), base64url."
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "registrationRequestId",
          "installationId",
          "displayName",
          "machineFingerprint",
          "publicKey",
          "keyProtection",
          "nonce",
          "signature"
        ],
        "description": "signature — подпись ключом publicKey строки \"sikkerheit-enroll-v1\\n<nonce>\\n<registrationRequestId>\\n<installationId>\\n<machineFingerprint>\\n<keyProtection>\\n<JWK thumbprint RFC 7638>\" в UTF-8. previousKeySignature — подпись той же строки текущим ключом устройства на сервере, когда агент меняет ключ, который у него ещё есть; без неё замена ключа идёт через разбор конфликта идентичности. keyProtection — заявление агента, для решений доверия не используется."
      },

        "type": "object",

          },

            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 80,
            "description": "Идентификатор часового пояса IANA; неизвестная зона — 422 validation_failed."
          },
          "reportLanguage": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "nb",
              "nn",
              "en"
            ]
          },
          "status": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "active",
              "disabled",
              "deletion_scheduled"
            ]
          },
          "deletionDueAt": {
            "type": [
              "string",
              "null"
            ],
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 40,
            "format": "date-time",
            "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?(Z|[+-][0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2})$"
          },
          "createdAt": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 40,
            "format": "date-time",
            "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?(Z|[+-][0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2})$"
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "id",
          "name",
          "timezone",
          "reportLanguage",
          "status",
          "deletionDueAt",
          "createdAt"
        ],
        "allOf": [
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "status": {
                  "const": "deletion_scheduled"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "status"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "deletionDueAt": {
                  "type": "string"
                }
              }
            },
            "else": {
              "properties": {
                "deletionDueAt": {
                  "type": "null"
                }
              }
            }
          }
        ]
      },

          },

            "type": "string",

      "OrganizationLifecycleAuditDetails": {

            "type": "string",

            "maxLength": 1000,

          }
        },
        "required": [
          "lifecycleAction",
          "reason",
          "exportWaived"

                  "const": false
                }
              }
            }
          }
        ]
      },

      "AuditEvent": {
        "type": "object",
        "additionalProperties": false,
        "properties": {
          "id": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 36,
            "format": "uuid",
            "pattern": "^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$"
          },
          "occurredAt": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 40,
            "format": "date-time",
            "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?(Z|[+-][0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2})$"
          },
          "actorType": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "staff",
              "device",
              "system"
            ]
          },
          "actorId": {
            "type": [
              "string",
              "null"
            ],
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 36,
            "format": "uuid",
            "pattern": "^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$",
            "description": "entraUserId при staff; deviceId при device; null при system."
          },
          "action": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 80,
            "pattern": "^[a-z0-9_]+(\\.[a-z0-9_]+)+$"
          },
          "targetType": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 40
          },
          "targetId": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 100
          },
          "outcome": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "success",
              "denied",
              "failed"
            ]
          },
          "correlationId": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 100
          },
          "details": {
            "type": [
              "object",
              "null"
            ]
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "id",
          "occurredAt",
          "actorType",
          "actorId",
          "action",
          "targetType",
          "targetId",
          "outcome",
          "correlationId",
          "details"
        ],
        "allOf": [
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "action": {
                  "const": "organization.lifecycle_change"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "action"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "details": {
                  "$ref": "#/components/schemas/OrganizationLifecycleAuditDetails"
                }
              }
            },
            "else": {
              "if": {
                "properties": {
                  "action": {
                    "const": "finding.assignment_change"
                  }
                },
                "required": [
                  "action"
                ]
              },
              "then": {
                "properties": {
                  "details": {
                    "$ref": "#/components/schemas/FindingAssignmentAuditDetails"
                  }
                }
              },
              "else": {
                "if": {
                  "properties": {
                    "action": {
                      "const": "retention.purge_summary"
                    }
                  },
                  "required": [
                    "action"
                  ]
                },
                "then": {
                  "properties": {
                    "details": {
                      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/RetentionPurgeAuditDetails"
                    }
                  }
                },
                "else": {
                  "properties": {
                    "details": {
                      "type": "null"
                    }
                  }
                }
              }
            }
          }
        ]
      },

        "description": "Закрытая конфигурация policy_revision. WIN-011 обязательна в activeCheckIds; assessed без входных настроек или для отложенной M365-007, отсутствие WIN-011 и активная WIN-004 без WIN-003 дают 422 policy_inputs_missing. maxBatchAgeDays >= maxBufferDays + 1, minGlobalAdmins <= maxGlobalAdmins, пороги warning WIN-001 не ниже critical и WIN-004 warnDays <= criticalDays, иначе 422 validation_failed. У каждого обязательного поля-значения есть default; политика v1 по умолчанию — значения default этой схемы."

          },
          "createdBy": {
            "type": [
              "string",

            ],
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 36,

            "pattern": "^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$"

          "configuration": {

        "required": [

          "configuration"

      "TenantConsentStart": {

          }

        "required": [

        ]
      },
      "ConsentStart": {
        "type": "object",

          },
          "startedAt": {
            "type": [
              "string",
              "null"
            ],
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 40,
            "format": "date-time",
            "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?(Z|[+-][0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2})$"
          },

              "string",

              "tenant_mismatch",
              "credential_error",
              "cancelled",
              "run_timeout",
              "temporary_error",
              null
            ],
            "description": "run_timeout — 30 минут истекли до чтения пользователей, или запуск открыт дольше 6 часов и закрыт плановым заданием (05, «Подключение M365 и сбор»)."

          },

            ],

      "Assessment": {
        "type": "object",
        "additionalProperties": false,
        "properties": {
          "id": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 36,
            "format": "uuid",
            "pattern": "^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$"
          },
          "subject": {
            "$ref": "#/components/schemas/Subject"
          },
          "checkId": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "M365-001",
              "M365-002",
              "M365-003",
              "M365-004",
              "M365-005",
              "M365-006",
              "M365-007",
              "WIN-001",
              "WIN-002",
              "WIN-003",
              "WIN-004",
              "WIN-005",
              "WIN-006",
              "WIN-007",
              "WIN-008",
              "WIN-009",
              "WIN-010",
              "WIN-011",
              "SYS-001",
              "SYS-002",
              "SYS-003"
            ]
          },
          "ruleId": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 80
          },
          "ruleVersion": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 40,
            "pattern": "^[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+(?:[-+][A-Za-z0-9.-]+)?$"
          },
          "sourcePolicyVersion": {
            "type": "integer",
            "minimum": 1
          },
          "evaluatedPolicyVersion": {
            "type": "integer",
            "minimum": 1
          },
          "assessmentScope": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "historical",
              "current"
            ]
          },
          "assessmentMode": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "assessed",
              "informational"
            ]
          },
          "result": {
            "type": [
              "string",
              "null"
            ],
            "enum": [
              "pass",
              "fail",
              "unknown",
              "not_applicable",
              null
            ]
          },
          "severity": {
            "type": [
              "string",
              "null"
            ],
            "enum": [
              "critical",
              "warning",
              null
            ]
          },
          "freshness": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "fresh",
              "stale"
            ]
          },
          "reasonCode": {
            "type": [
              "string",
              "null"
            ],
            "enum": [
              "permission_denied",
              "unsupported",
              "collector_timeout",
              "temporary_error",
              "license_missing",
              "partial_data",
              "missing_observation",
              "clock_inconsistent",
              "unmapped_state",
              "applicability_input_missing",
              "policy_not_applicable",
              "evidence_time_invalid",
              "os_version_unknown",
              "os_inventory_missing",
              "release_catalog_stale",
              "build_not_in_catalog",
              "enforcement_unconfirmed",
              null
            ]
          },
          "observedAt": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 40,
            "format": "date-time",
            "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?(Z|[+-][0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2})$"
          },
          "receivedAt": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 40,
            "format": "date-time",
            "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?(Z|[+-][0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2})$"
          },
          "evidence": {
            "type": [
              "object",
              "null"
            ]
          },
          "evidenceAvailability": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "available",
              "expired"
            ]
          },
          "explanation": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 2000
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "id",
          "subject",
          "checkId",
          "ruleId",
          "ruleVersion",
          "sourcePolicyVersion",
          "evaluatedPolicyVersion",
          "assessmentScope",
          "assessmentMode",
          "result",
          "severity",
          "freshness",
          "reasonCode",
          "observedAt",
          "receivedAt",
          "evidence",
          "evidenceAvailability",
          "explanation"
        ],
        "allOf": [
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "assessmentMode": {
                  "const": "informational"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "assessmentMode"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "result": {
                  "type": "null"
                },
                "severity": {
                  "type": "null"
                },
                "reasonCode": {
                  "type": [
                    "string",
                    "null"
                  ],
                  "enum": [
                    "permission_denied",
                    "unsupported",
                    "collector_timeout",
                    "temporary_error",
                    "license_missing",
                    "partial_data",
                    "missing_observation",
                    "clock_inconsistent",
                    "applicability_input_missing",
                    null
                  ]
                }
              }
            },
            "else": {
              "properties": {
                "result": {
                  "type": "string"
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "result": {
                  "enum": [
                    "pass",
                    "fail"
                  ]
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "result"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "reasonCode": {
                  "type": "null"
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "result": {
                  "const": "fail"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "result"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "severity": {
                  "type": "string"
                }
              }
            },
            "else": {
              "properties": {
                "severity": {
                  "type": "null"
                }
              }
            }
          }
        ],
        "description": "Режим informational: result и severity всегда null, finding не создаётся. reasonCode допускается при result unknown или not_applicable, а у informational — только переносимые причины отсутствия данных (03, «Семантика результата»)."
      },

              "null"
            ],
            "enum": [
              "new",
              "in_progress",
              "risk_accepted",
              "suppressed",
              null
            ]
          },
          "toWorkflowState": {
            "type": [
              "string",
              "null"
            ],
            "enum": [
              "new",
              "in_progress",
              "risk_accepted",
              "suppressed",
              null
            ]
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "id",
          "occurredAt",
          "eventType",
          "cause",
          "assessmentId",
          "actorId",
          "fromWorkflowState",
          "toWorkflowState"
        ],
        "allOf": [
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "eventType": {
                  "const": "opened"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "eventType"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "cause": {
                  "enum": [
                    "source_change",
                    "rule_change",
                    "policy_change"
                  ]
                },
                "assessmentId": {
                  "type": "string"
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "eventType": {
                  "const": "resolved"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "eventType"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "cause": {
                  "enum": [
                    "source_change",
                    "rule_change",
                    "policy_change",
                    "applicability_change"
                  ]
                },
                "assessmentId": {
                  "type": "string"
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "eventType": {
                  "const": "reopened"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "eventType"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "cause": {
                  "enum": [
                    "source_change",
                    "rule_change",
                    "policy_change"
                  ]
                },
                "assessmentId": {
                  "type": "string"
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "eventType": {
                  "const": "quality_changed"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "eventType"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "cause": {
                  "enum": [
                    "data_quality"
                  ]
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "eventType": {
                  "const": "workflow_changed"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "eventType"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "cause": {
                  "enum": [
                    "manual_action",
                    "review_expired"
                  ]
                },
                "assessmentId": {
                  "type": "null"
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "eventType": {
                  "const": "policy_changed"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "eventType"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "cause": {
                  "enum": [
                    "policy_change"
                  ]
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "eventType": {
                  "const": "subject_retired"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "eventType"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "cause": {
                  "enum": [
                    "source_change",
                    "manual_action"
                  ]
                },
                "assessmentId": {
                  "type": "null"
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "cause": {
                  "const": "manual_action"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "cause"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "actorId": {
                  "type": "string"
                }
              }
            },
            "else": {
              "properties": {
                "actorId": {
                  "type": "null"
                }
              }
            }
          }
        ]
      },

            ]
          },

            "type": "string",

            "maxLength": 1000
          },

            "type": "string",

            "maxLength": 40,

          }
        },
        "required": [

        ],
        "allOf": [
          {
            "if": {

                }
              },

            "then": {
              "not": {

              ]

      "ReportCreate": {
        "type": "object",

        "properties": {

          },
          "periodEnd": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 40,

              "html"

          }
        },
        "required": [

          "periodEnd",
          "format"
        ],
        "description": "Только завершённая ISO-неделя в timezone организации: periodStart — понедельник 00:00, periodEnd — следующий понедельник 00:00 не позже текущего момента; иные границы, текущая и будущая неделя — 422 validation_failed (05, «Отчёт и покрытие»)."
      },
      "Report": {
        "type": "object",
        "additionalProperties": false,
        "properties": {

            "type": "string",

          "timezone",
          "language",

          "format",
          "failureCode"

        "description": "Отчёт ready неизменяем: снимок, записанный при генерации, не переписывается, а потерянный файл строится заново из снимка. Отчёт failed повторный createReport ставит в генерацию заново (05, «Отчёт и покрытие»)."
      },
```
