                  "maxLength": 40,
                  "format": "date-time",
                  "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?(Z|[+-][0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2})$"
                },
                "kind": {
                  "type": "string",
                  "enum": [
                    "security_monthly",
                    "security_oob",
                    "nonsecurity_oob",
                    "preview",
                    "hotpatch"
                  ]
                },
                "securityMonth": {
                  "type": [
                    "string",
                    "null"
                  ],
                  "minLength": 7,
                  "maxLength": 7,
                  "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$"
                },
                "autoOffered": {
                  "type": "boolean"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "branchBuild",
                "ubr",
                "kb",
                "releaseAt",
                "kind",
                "securityMonth",
                "autoOffered"
              ]
            },
            "minItems": 0,
            "maxItems": 100
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "source",
          "securityMonth"
        ],
        "allOf": [
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "source": {
                  "const": "manual"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "source"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "required": [
                "entries"
              ]
            },
            "else": {
              "not": {
                "required": [
                  "entries"
                ]
              }
            }
          }
        ],
        "description": "Создаёт записи в состоянии draft; в оценку они попадают только после verifyWindowsReleaseMonth."
      },
      "WindowsReleaseMonth": {
        "type": "object",
        "additionalProperties": false,
        "properties": {
          "securityMonth": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 7,
            "maxLength": 7,
            "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$"
          },
          "state": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "draft",
              "verified"
            ]
          },
          "releases": {
            "type": "integer",
            "minimum": 0
          },
          "verifiedAt": {
            "type": [
              "string",
              "null"
            ],
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 40,
            "format": "date-time",
            "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?(Z|[+-][0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2})$"
          },
          "verifiedBy": {
            "type": [
              "string",
              "null"
            ],
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 36,
            "format": "uuid",
            "pattern": "^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$"
          },
          "noNewReleases": {
            "type": "boolean"
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "securityMonth",
          "state",
          "releases",
          "verifiedAt",
          "verifiedBy",
          "noNewReleases"
        ]
      },
      "WindowsReleaseMonthVerification": {
        "type": "object",
        "additionalProperties": false,
        "properties": {
          "noNewReleases": {
            "type": "boolean",
            "default": false,
            "description": "Подтвердить месяц без новых записей (например, только внеочередные выпуски не для клиентов)."
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "noNewReleases"
        ]
      },
      "DeviceUpdate": {
        "type": "object",
        "additionalProperties": false,
        "properties": {
          "displayName": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 120,
            "pattern": "^[^\\u0000-\\u001F\\u007F-\\u009F​-‏‪-‮⁦-⁩<>]+$"
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "displayName"
        ]
      },
      "ScanRequestCreate": {
        "type": "object",
        "additionalProperties": false,
        "properties": {
          "idempotencyKey": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 36,
            "format": "uuid",
            "pattern": "^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$"
          },
          "reason": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "verify_remediation"
            ]
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "idempotencyKey",
          "reason"
        ]
      },
      "ScanRequest": {
        "type": "object",
        "additionalProperties": false,
        "properties": {
          "id": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 36,
            "format": "uuid",
            "pattern": "^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$"
          },
          "state": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "requested",
              "delivered",
              "completed",
              "expired"
            ]
          },
          "expiresAt": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 40,
            "format": "date-time",
            "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?(Z|[+-][0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2})$"
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "id",
          "state",
          "expiresAt"
        ]
      },
      "OrganizationOverviewPage": {
        "type": "object",
        "additionalProperties": false,
        "properties": {
          "items": {

Файл `specs/scan-batch.schema.json`

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "$id": "https://contracts.sikkerheit.example.invalid/agent/scan-batch.schema.json",
  "title": "Sikkerheit Agent Scan Batch",
  "type": "object",
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "properties": {
    "schemaVersion": {
      "const": "2.0"
    },
    "scanId": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 36,
      "format": "uuid",
      "pattern": "^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$"
    },
    "sequence": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1
    },
    "agentVersion": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 40,
      "pattern": "^[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+(?:[-+][A-Za-z0-9.-]+)?$"
    },
    "policyVersion": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "description": "Версия политики источника."
    },
    "machineFingerprint": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 64,
      "pattern": "^[0-9a-f]{64}$"
    },
    "startedAt": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 40,
      "format": "date-time",
      "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?Z$"
    },
    "completedAt": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 40,
      "format": "date-time",
      "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?Z$"
    },
    "scanRequestId": {
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ],
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 36,
      "format": "uuid",
      "pattern": "^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$",
      "description": "Чужой или просроченный scanRequestId не отклоняет пакет: он принимается как обычный сбор, scanRequestId игнорируется, пишется запись диагностики."
    },
    "observations": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "$ref": "observation.schema.json"
      },
      "minItems": 1,
      "maxItems": 200,
      "description": "Ключ (checkId, subject.type, localKey) уникален внутри пакета; localKey = device для устройства и volumeId для тома. Повтор ключа: 422 duplicate_observation."
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "schemaVersion",
    "scanId",
    "sequence",
    "agentVersion",
    "policyVersion",
    "machineFingerprint",
    "startedAt",
    "completedAt",
    "observations"
  ],
  "description": "DeviceId и OrganizationId определяются сессией устройства, а не телом пакета. Лимит байтов, idempotency, sequence, окна и время проверяются сервером дополнительно (05, «Порядок приёма пакета»). Пакет с completedAt раньше startedAt или observedAt вне [startedAt, completedAt] отклоняется 422 batch_time_inconsistent. Время отправки передаётся заголовком Sikkerheit-Sent-At каждой попытки и в тело не входит, поэтому повтор отправляет те же байты (05, «Время и часы устройства»)."
}
```
